  <div  class="popup-content">
    
<form class="search-form" role="search" method="get" id="" action="https://www.group-ib.com/">
  <input type="search" placeholder="Search" name="s" id="search-input" value=""/>
  <button type="submit" class="search-button"><img src="/wp-content/themes/gib-theme/assets/images/search-icon.svg" alt="Search Icon"></button>
</form>   </div>

<style>
  .hidden {
    display: none;
  }
</style>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
  const openSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.open-search-popup')];
  const closeSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.close-search-popup')];
  const searchPopup = document.querySelector('.search-popup');

  const closePopup = () => {
    searchPopup.style.display = 'none';
    openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.add('hidden'));
  };

  openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(openSearchPopupBtn => {
    openSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();

      searchPopup.style.display = 'block';
      openSearchPopupBtn.classList.add('hidden');
      closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    });
  });

  closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(closeSearchPopupBtn => {
    closeSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      closePopup();
    });
  });

  document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
    if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target) && !openSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target)) && !closeSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target))) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target)) {
          closePopup();
        }
      }, 100);
    }
  });
</script>    </div>

                            <h1 class="header__title title-h2 mb-24 mt-md-48 mt-lg-64 mt-xl-64">New attacks by UltraRank group</h1><p class="header__text mb-xs-48 mb-64 mb-lg-96 mb-xl-96">As part of UltraRank's new campaign, Group-IB Threat Intelligence team discovered 12 eCommerce websites infected with their JavaScript-sniffer.</p><div class="post-info__date mb-24 mb-lg-32 mb-xl-48"><div class="body2">December 23, 2020 · <span id="reading-time"></span> min to read · Threat Intelligence</div></div><div class="post-info__image mb-24 mb-lg-32 mb-xl-48"><picture><source srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/img-1440-34.jpg.webp" type="image/webp"><img data-no-lazy="" fetchpriority="high" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/img-1440-34.jpg" alt="" class="webpexpress-processed"></picture></div><div class="post-info__tags"><div class="post-info__tag body2">JS sniffers</div><div class="post-info__tag body2">Threat Intelligence</div><div class="post-info__tag body2">UltraRank</div></div>                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="blog-post-content" id="blog-post-content">
                    <div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><h2>Introduction</h2>
<div>
<p>In August 2020, Group-IB published the report <a href="/resources/research-hub/ultrarank/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">&#8220;UltraRank: the unexpected twist of a JS-sniffer triple threat&#8221;</a>. The report described the operations of the cybercriminal group <strong>UltraRank</strong>, which in five years of activity had successfully attacked 691 eCommerce stores and 13 website service providers.</p>
<p>In November 2020, Group-IB experts discovered a new <strong>wave of UltraRank attacks</strong>. Even though new attacks were detected at the time, part of the group&#8217;s infrastructure remained active and some sites were still infected. The cybercriminals did not use existing domains for new attacks but switched to a new infrastructure to store malicious code and collect intercepted payment data.</p>
<p>As part of UltraRank&#8217;s new campaign, Group-IB <a href="/products/threat-intelligence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Threat Intelligence</a> team discovered 12 eCommerce websites infected with their JavaScript-sniffer. Eight of them remain infected at the moment of publication. Group-IB has sent notifications to the infected websites.</p>
<p>This time the JS sniffer&#8217;s code was obfuscated using <strong>Radix</strong> obfuscation. This obfuscation pattern had been used by only a few cybercriminal groups, one of which was the UltraRank group (Figure 1). After deobfuscating the code, Group-IB found that the attacks used a sniffer from the <strong>SnifLite</strong> family, already known to Group-IB experts and used by the threat actor UltraRank. Due to the relatively small number of infected websites, the attackers most likely used the credentials in the CMS administrative panel, which, in turn, could have been compromised using malware or as a result of brute force attacks.</p>
<p>During their most recent series of attacks UltraRank stored their malicious code on the website mimicking a legitimate Google Tag Manager domain. The analysis of the threat actor&#8217;s infrastructure revealed that the main server was hosted by Media Land LLC, which is connected with a bullet-proof hosting company.</p>
<p>This blog post examines UltraRank&#8217;s new campaign, provides recommendations to banks, payment systems, and online merchants. You&#8217;ll also find indicators of compromise, attackers&#8217; TTPs and relevant mitigation and defense techniques in accordance with MITRE ATT&amp;CK and MITRE Shield that we recommend to use to protect against UltraRank.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div id="attachment_26522" style="width: 984px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1-min-20.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26522" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26522 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20974%20400'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Fragment of the obfuscated sniffer code" width="974" height="400" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1-min-20.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26522" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26522 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1-min-20.png" alt="Fragment of the obfuscated sniffer code" width="974" height="400"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26522" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 1: Fragment of the obfuscated sniffer code</p></div>
<h2>Analysis of the JS Sniffer code</h2>
<p>The SnifLite JS sniffer family has been used by UltraRank since at least January 2019, when it was utilized in an <a href="https://www.trendmicro.com/en_us/research/19/a/new-magecart-attack-delivered-through-compromised-advertising-supply-chain.html" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">attack</a> on the Adverline advertising network. Malicious code is uploaded to the infected website by a link to a JS file located on the website <em>hXXp://googletagsmanager[.]co/</em>, the domain disguised as a legitimate domain of the Google Tag Manager googletagmanager.com. The cybercriminals&#8217; website <em>hXXp://googletagsmanager[.]co/</em> is also used to collect intercepted payment card data as a sniffer gate (Figure 2).</p>
<div id="attachment_26523" style="width: 984px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2-min-26.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26523" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26523 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20974%20247'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Fragment of the deobfuscated JS sniffer code" width="974" height="247" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2-min-26.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26523" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26523 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2-min-26.png" alt="Fragment of the deobfuscated JS sniffer code" width="974" height="247"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26523" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 2: Fragment of the deobfuscated JS sniffer code with a link to the gate to collect intercepted cards</p></div>
<p>The function responsible for intercepting payment information in the SnifLite sniffer family is shown in Figure 3. The data collection algorithm is based on the function querySelectorAll, like in the FakeLogistics and WebRank sniffer families used by the group earlier. A comparison of these three families was outlined in the report &#8220;UltraRank: the unexpected twist of a JS-sniffer triple threat.&#8221;</p>
<p>After data is collected, it is written to local storage in an object named <em>google.verify.cache.001</em>.</p>
<div id="attachment_26529" style="width: 984px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/3-min-27.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26529" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26529 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20974%20366'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Fragment of the JS sniffer code" width="974" height="366" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/3-min-27.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26529" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26529 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/3-min-27.png" alt="Fragment of the JS sniffer code" width="974" height="366"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26529" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 3: Fragment of the JS sniffer code with a function responsible for collecting payment card data</p></div>
<p>Data is collected and sent only if the current address of the page where the user is located contains one of the following keywords (Figure 4):</p>
<ul>
<li>onepage</li>
<li>checkout</li>
<li>store</li>
<li>cart</li>
<li>pay</li>
<li>panier</li>
<li>kasse</li>
<li>order</li>
<li>billing</li>
<li>purchase</li>
<li>basket</li>
</ul>
<p>Before sending an intercepted payment card, its data is extracted from the <em>_google.verify.cache.001object</em> stored locally and transmitted to the cybercriminals by sending an HTTP GET request.</p>
<div id="attachment_26521" style="width: 984px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/4-min-27.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26521" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26521 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20974%20335'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Fragment of the JS sniffer code" width="974" height="335" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/4-min-27.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26521" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26521 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/4-min-27.png" alt="Fragment of the JS sniffer code" width="974" height="335"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26521" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 4: Fragment of the JS sniffer code with the function to send the collected data to the cybercriminals&#8217; server</p></div>
<p>During further analysis of infections by UltraRank, Group-IB team discovered a sample of a JS sniffer without obfuscation, identical to what was found on one of the cybercriminals&#8217; websites earlier, which linked UltraRank to the new attacks.</p>
<h2>Analysis of the infrastructure</h2>
<p>While analyzing the sniffer infrastructure, a standard PHP script was found, which is typical of all of UltraRank&#8217;s websites. In addition to the common information about the sent request and the server, the script displayed the server&#8217;s real IP address. At the time of analysis, the googletagsmanager[.]co domain had an IP address of <em>8.208.16[.]230</em> (AS45102, Alibaba (US) Technology Co., Ltd.). At the same time, the real server address was <em>45.141.84[.]239</em> (Figure 5), owned by Media Land LLC (AS206728). According to an <a href="https://krebsonsecurity.com/2019/07/meet-the-worlds-biggest-bulletproof-hoster/" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">article</a> by Brian Krebs, Media Land LLC is connected with a bulletproof hosting company operated by an underground forum user going by the nickname Yalishanda, which provides services to cybercriminals. Presumably, Yalishanda&#8217;s service uses cloud servers rented from various suppliers, including Alibaba, to host part of the cybercriminals&#8217; infrastructure.</p>
<p>In addition to the server IP address, the script output also specifies the directory where the website files are located on the server <em>hXXp://googletagsmanager[.]co/</em>: <strong>worker</strong>.</p>
<div id="attachment_26528" style="width: 984px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29.png.webp 974w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29-150x150.png.webp 150w" sizes="(max-width: 974px) 100vw, 974px" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26528" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26528 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20974%20972'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Script output - ultrarank" width="974" height="972" data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29.png 974w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29-150x150.png 150w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 974px) 100vw, 974px" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26528" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26528 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29.png" alt="Script output - ultrarank" width="974" height="972" srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29.png 974w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5-min-29-150x150.png 150w" sizes="(max-width: 974px) 100vw, 974px"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26528" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 5: Script output with information about the server where the domain googletagsmanager.co is located</p></div>
<p>The IP address <em>45.141.84[.]239</em> is also linked to the website <em>hXXp://s-panel[.]su/</em>. During its analysis, the same script on all websites in UltraRank&#8217;s infrastructure was found again (Figure 6). In this case, the directory where all the website files were located is called <strong>panel</strong>.</p>
<div id="attachment_26527" style="width: 984px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/6-min-21.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26527" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26527 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20974%20945'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Script output - ultrarank" width="974" height="945" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/6-min-21.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26527" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26527 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/6-min-21.png" alt="Script output - ultrarank" width="974" height="945"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26527" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 6: Script output with information about the server where the domain s-panel.su is located</p></div>
<p>In addition to the common server, Group-IB&#8217;s <a href="/products/threat-intelligence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Graph Network Analysis</a> system detected the SSL certificate <em>50e15969b10d40388bffbb87f56dd83df14576af</em>. This certificate was on both the domain googletagsmanager.co and the server with the IP address <em>45.141.84[.]239</em>, which is associated with the domain <em>s-panel[.]su</em> (Figure 7).</p>
<div id="attachment_26525" style="width: 984px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23.png.webp 974w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23-150x150.png.webp 150w" sizes="(max-width: 974px) 100vw, 974px" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26525" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26525 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20974%20981'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Certificate Link graph - ultrarank" width="974" height="981" data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23.png 974w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23-150x150.png 150w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 974px) 100vw, 974px" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26525" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26525 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23.png" alt="Certificate Link graph - ultrarank" width="974" height="981" srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23.png 974w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7-min-23-150x150.png 150w" sizes="(max-width: 974px) 100vw, 974px"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26525" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 7: Certificate Link graph 50e15969b10d40388bffbb87f56dd83df14576af from Group-IB Threat Intelligence system</p></div>
<p>Throughout further analysis of the website <em>hXXp://s-panel[.]su/</em>, a login form was detected. Presumably, this website is used by the cybercriminals as a sniffer control panel: all stolen payment card data is collected in the panel for subsequent exfiltration and resale.</p>
<div id="attachment_26526" style="width: 480px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/8-min-22.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26526" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26526 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20470%20200'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Login form found on the phishing site " width="470" height="200" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/8-min-22.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-26526" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-26526 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/8-min-22.png" alt="Login form found on the phishing site " width="470" height="200"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-26526" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 8: Login form found on the site s-panel.su</p></div>
<p>The googletagsmanager[.]info domain was also discovered. In September 2020, this domain had the same IP address as <em>googletagsmanager[.]co</em> (8.208.96.88). However, at the time of writing, the website was inactive and no cases of eCommerce infections using it were found.</p>
<h3>Indicators of compromise</h3>
<ul>
<li>googletagsmanager[.]co</li>
<li>googletagsmanager[.]info</li>
<li>s-panel[.]su</li>
</ul>
<h2>Recommendations</h2>
<p>To date, Group-IB experts have studied 96 different JS sniffer families, whereas only 38 malware families of this type were known when the report &#8220;<a href="https://www.group-ib.com/resources/research-hub/js-sniffers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Crime without punishment: in-depth analysis of JS sniffers</a>&#8221; was published. <strong>Attacks on eCommerce stores using malicious JavaScript are becoming an increasingly popular way to obtain large amounts of user payment information for subsequent resale</strong>. As a result of UltraRank installing malicious code on the Ticketmaster website by hacking the third-party provider Inbenta, user payment data was leaked. Ticketmaster was fined £1.25 million for this. In addition, British Airways was fined £20 million for a data leak caused by malicious code injected in one of the JavaScript libraries used on their website and mobile app. Therefore, <strong>the threat of JS sniffers is relevant not only for owners of eCommerce stores, but also for all services that use and process bank card payments online.</strong> Group-IB experts have compiled a list of recommendations that will help various eCommerce participants minimize potential damage, prevent infection, or detect existing malicious activity.</p>
</div>
</div>
                            
                            <div class="blog-post-content__section"><div class="accordionList173"><div class="accordion-item"><div class="accordion-item__title"><div class="title-h5">For issuing banks</div><div class="accordion-item__icon"><span class="material-icons">arrow_drop_down</span></div></div><div class="accordion-item__text"><ul>
<li>Notify users of possible risks arising in the online payment process when using payment cards.</li>
<li>If payment cards related to your bank have been compromised, block these cards and notify the users that the eCommerce store has been infected with a payment card sniffer.</li>
</ul>
</div></div><div class="accordion-item"><div class="accordion-item__title"><div class="title-h5">For eCommerce websites administrators</div><div class="accordion-item__icon"><span class="material-icons">arrow_drop_down</span></div></div><div class="accordion-item__text"><ul>
<li>Use complex and unique passwords to access the website&#8217;s admin panel and any services used for administration, for example phpMyAdmin, Adminer. If possible, set up two-factor authentication.</li>
<li>Install all necessary updates for the software used, including CMS of websites. Do not use outdated or unsupported versions of the CMS. This will help to reduce the risk of servers being compromised and make it more difficult for an attacker to download a <a href="https://www.group-ib.com/resources/knowledge-hub/web-shell/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">web shell</a> and install malicious code.</li>
<li>Regularly check the store for malware and conduct regular security audits of your website. For example, for websites based on CMS Magento, you can use Magento Security Scan Tool.</li>
<li>Use the appropriate systems to log all changes that occur on the website, as well as to log access to the website&#8217;s control panel and database and track file change dates. This will help you to detect website files infected with malicious code, as well as track unauthorized access to the website or web server.</li>
</ul>
</div></div><div class="accordion-item"><div class="accordion-item__title"><div class="title-h5">For payment systems/payment processing banks</div><div class="accordion-item__icon"><span class="material-icons">arrow_drop_down</span></div></div><div class="accordion-item__text"><ul>
<li>If you provide payment services for eCommerce websites, regularly inform your customers about basic security techniques when accepting online payments on the websites, as well as the threat of JavaScript sniffers;</li>
<li>Ensure that your services use a correctly-configured Content Security Policy.</li>
</ul>
</div></div>
                      <script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
                        jQuery(window).on('load', function () {
                          let accordion173 = document.querySelector('.accordionList173');
                          if (accordion173) {
                            let items = accordion173.querySelectorAll('.accordion-item');
                            let title = accordion173.querySelectorAll('.accordion-item__title');

                            function toggleAccordion() {
                              let thisItem = this.parentNode;
                              items.forEach((item) => {
                                if (thisItem == item) {
                                  thisItem.classList.toggle('active');
                                  return;
                                }
                                item.classList.remove('active');
                              });
                            }

                            title.forEach((question) => question.addEventListener('click', toggleAccordion));
                          } 
                        });
                      </script>
                      </div></div><div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><p><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/9-min-5.jpg.webp" type="image/webp"><img decoding="async" class="alignnone size-full wp-image-26524 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201500%201352'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="UltraRank MITRE ATT&CK and MITRE Shield" width="1500" height="1352" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/9-min-5.jpg"><noscript><img decoding="async" class="alignnone size-full wp-image-26524 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/9-min-5.jpg" alt="UltraRank MITRE ATT&CK and MITRE Shield" width="1500" height="1352"></noscript></picture></p>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    const postcontent = document.getElementById('blog-post-content');
    const h2counter = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2');

    for (i = 0; i < h2counter.length; i++) {
        const h2elementtag = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i];
        const h2element = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i].innerHTML;
        let postnavitem = document.createElement('a');
        postnavitem.classList.add('post-navigation__item', 'subtitle2', 'scrollto');
        postnavitem.innerHTML = h2element;
        const postnavlink = postnavitem.innerHTML;
        let str = postnavlink;
        str = str.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s\u0400-\u04FF]/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/\s+/g, '-').toLowerCase();
        str = str.replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
        postnavitem.classList += ' ' + str;
        postnavitem.href = '#' + str;
        h2elementtag.id = str;
        document.getElementById('post-navigation__wrapper').appendChild(postnavitem);
    }

    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('h2');
    const navItems = document.querySelectorAll('.post-navigation__item');

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        let current = '';
        sections.forEach((section) => {
            const sectionTop = section.offsetTop;
            const sectionHeight = section.clientHeight;
            if (pageYOffset >= sectionTop - sectionHeight / 10) {
                current = section.getAttribute('id');
            }
        });

        navItems.forEach((a) => {
            a.classList.remove('active');
            if (a.classList.contains(current)) {
                a.classList.add('active');
            }
        });
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrollto').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300,
        );

        return false;
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL WITH NO HASH
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrolltonohash').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300
        );

        return false;
    });
</script>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    function readingTime() {
        const text = document.getElementById("blog-post-content").innerText;
        const wpm = 225;
        const words = text.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
        const time = Math.ceil(words / wpm);
        document.getElementById("reading-time").innerText = time;
    }
    readingTime();
</script>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    document.getElementById("share-url").value = window.location.href;
    document.getElementById("share-url-mobile").value = window.location.href;
    jQuery('.share-url__copy').click(function() {
        jQuery('.share-url__copy').addClass('success');
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
        setTimeout(() => {
            jQuery('.share-url__copy').removeClass('success');
        }, 1500);
    });
</script>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    var carousel123 = new Swiper('.carousel123', {
        grabCursor: true,
        pagination: {
            el: '.swiper-pagination',
            clickable: true,
        },
    });

    var authorsMoreThan2Swiper = new Swiper('.authors-more-than-2-swiper', {
        grabCursor: true,
        pagination: {
            el: '.swiper-pagination',
            clickable: true,
        },
        breakpoints: {
            0: {
                slidesPerView: 'auto',
                spaceBetween: 16,
                slidesPerView: 1,
                slidesPerGroup: 1,
            },
            620: {
                spaceBetween: 24,
                slidesPerView: 2,
                slidesPerGroup: 2,
            },
        },
    });

    function positioningSidebar() {
        let sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        let sidebarHeight = sidebar.offsetHeight;
        let windowHeight = document.documentElement.clientHeight;
        if (windowHeight < (sidebarHeight + 48)) {
            let sidebarAuthors = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__authors');
            let sidebarMorePosts = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__more-posts');
            let authorsHeight = sidebarAuthors ? sidebarAuthors.offsetHeight : 0;
            let morePostsHeight = sidebarMorePosts ? sidebarMorePosts.offsetHeight : 0;
            let sidebarTop = authorsHeight + morePostsHeight + 140;
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', -sidebarTop + 'px');
        } else {
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', '72px');
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', positioningSidebar);
    positioningSidebar();

    if (localStorage.getItem('theme')) {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
        } else if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'light') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--white');
        }
    } else {
        localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
    }

    jQuery('.theme-toggle').click(function() {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
        } else {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        }
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--dark');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--white');
        setTimeout(() => {
            jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        }, 250);
    });
    jQuery('.share-toggle').click(function() {
        jQuery('.share-toggle').toggleClass('active');
        jQuery('.share-widget').toggleClass('show');
    });
</script>
